|   |   |   |-- checkers                                                                   │ Checkers strategy game - board game with jumping, crowning, piece movement
|   |   |   |-- chicago                                                                    │ Chicago dice game - specific dice rule format with phase management
|   |   |   |-- cho-han                                                                    │ Cho-han Japanese dice game - even/odd betting on two-dice roll
|   |   |   |-- connect-four                                                               │ Connect Four strategy game - gravity-based column drops, four-in-a-row wins
|   |   |   |-- crossclimb                                                                 │ Crossclimb puzzle game - tile matching with gravity and cascading mechanics
|   |   |   |-- farkle                                                                     │ Farkle dice game - 6-dice rolling with category scoring and risk management
|   |   |   |-- hangman                                                                    │ Hangman word-guessing game - letter selection with wrong-guess counting
|   |   |   |-- liars-dice                                                                 │ Liars Dice bluffing game - hidden dice with bid/challenge mechanics
|   |   |   |-- lights-out                                                                 │ Lights Out logic puzzle - grid toggling with cascading effects
|   |   |   |-- mancala                                                                    │ Mancala strategy game - pit-based stone distribution and capture
|   |   |   |-- memory-game                                                                │ Memory card matching game - tile flipping with pair matching
|   |   |   |-- mexico                                                                     │ Mexico dice game - two-dice combination rolling with hand history
|   |   |   |-- minesweeper                                                                │ Minesweeper logic puzzle - mine detection with number grids and auto-clear
|   |   |   |-- mini-sudoku                                                                │ Mini Sudoku 4x4 puzzle - simplified 4x4 Sudoku with digit placement
|   |   |   |-- monchola                                                                   │ Monchola card game variant - specific card combination and phase rules
|   |   |   |-- nim                                                                        │ Nim strategy game - object removal with last-object penalty or victory
|   |   |   |-- pig                                                                        │ Pig dice game - simple roll-accumulate with 1-risk penalty
|   |   |   |-- pinpoint                                                                   │ Pinpoint targeting game - coordinate guessing with proximity feedback
|   |   |   |-- queens                                                                     │ N-Queens puzzle - queen placement with no-conflict constraint solving
|   |   |   |-- reversi                                                                    │ Reversi (Othello) strategy game - disc flipping with color control
|   |   |   |-- rock-paper-scissors                                                        │ Rock Paper Scissors decision game - hand gesture with win/loss/draw
